Conclusion: Ptah框架通过结构化多智能体协作和视觉工作记忆机制，有效解决了多模态深度研究中的可验证性和图文交织问题，为生成高质量多模态报告提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced autonomous agents from deep search, which retrieves concise factual answers, to deep research, which synthesizes scattered evidence into long-form reports. However, verifiable multimodal deep research remains challenging due to open-ended synthesis without deterministic ground truth and the need to interleave textual arguments with visual evidence. We propose \textsc{Ptah}, a multi-agent harness for interleaved report generation. \textsc{Ptah} orchestrates the lifecycle from user query to rendered web report through planning, research, and writing stages, where specialized agents construct visual-aware plans, collect claim-grounded evidence, maintain source-aligned images in a \textit{Visual Working Memory}, and compose reports through declarative multimodal tool use. A verifier agent serves as the harness's acceptance function, enforcing factual grounding, citation fidelity, and cross-modal consistency throughout the workflow. We further introduce \textsc{Ptah}Eval, an evaluation protocol that augments existing benchmarks with image-level and presentation-level assessments. Experiments on deep research benchmarks show that \textsc{Ptah} produces more reliable, visually informative, and usable human-facing multimodal reports than strong baselines.

</details>


### [186] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao,Ziwei Zhang,Chuanyue Yu,Xingcheng Fu,Qingyun Sun,Runhua Xu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRITIC-R1 提出了一种基于强化学习的结构化批评框架，将RAG错误诊断显式建模为多维度分类问题，通过保守判断对齐和诊断质量对齐两种奖励函数缓解过度干预问题，显著提升了RAG系统的答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法仍存在幻觉和细粒度推理错误；引入的外部批评者提供的反馈粗粒度、弱结构化、过度干预，导致噪声大、不可靠的修正。

Method: 将RAG批评建模为显式错误诊断问题，包含判决、错误位置、推理分析和修复生成四个维度；设计CJA和DQA两种奖励函数分别缓解过度干预和提升细粒度诊断；采用GRPO+RL训练，过程级监督来自外部LLM教师模型。

Result: 在5个QA基准上，CRITIC-R1相比强RAG基线持续提升了答案质量。

Conclusion: 结构化、多维度的批评框架结合强化学习能有效提升RAG系统的修正效果，缓解现有批评方法的过度干预和反馈噪声问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines.
  Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/critic-r1-FCB0

</details>


### [187] [Internal Representation, Not Clinical Knowledge: Where Apparent LLM Triage Failures Originate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29889)
*David Fraile Navarro,Berardino Como,Jialei Sheng,Soundariya Ananthan,Shlomo Berkovsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，患者视角的临床分诊基准测试中，大型语言模型在受约束的多选输出格式下出现高低估（under-triage）率，但相同病例在自由文本格式下评分不同。通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征分析，证明问题源于输出格式而非临床表征——相同的医学特征在两种格式下均被激活，但在多选决策标记处“静默”，且决策由格式特征而非医学特征驱动。行为实验进一步证实，多选惩罚在结构化和自然语言输入下均存在，且错误主要由相邻选项偏移而非知识缺失导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在临床分诊任务中，输出格式（多选 vs. 自由文本）是否改变模型的临床表征，还是仅影响从表征到答案的映射过程。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析Gemma 3 4B/12B IT和Qwen3-8B模型在两种输出格式下的神经特征活动，结合自然语言自编码器口头化、决策标记logit归因和顶级特征表征三种独立方法，以及行为实验（选项顺序打乱、结构化输入测试）。

Result: 医学特征在两种格式下均激活，但在多选决策标记处“静默”；决策logit由支架/格式特征而非医学特征驱动；多选惩罚在结构化和自然语言输入下均存在；错误主要由相邻选项偏移（off-by-one）导致，而非知识失败。

Conclusion: 高低估率问题根源于输出格式（多选）而非临床表征，模型保留了正确的临床知识但无法在受限格式中正确映射到答案。

Abstract: Patient-voiced clinical-triage benchmarks report high under-triage rates for consumer LLMs for constrained multiple-choice output, yet the same cases score differently with free-text. We ask whether output format changes the model's \emph{clinical representation} or only the mapping from a preserved representation to an answer. Using sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features in Gemma 3 4B/12B IT and Qwen3-8B, we find the same medical features fire on the shared clinical narrative under both formats but go {silent} at the multiple-choice decision token in all the cases at every model. Three independent methods (natural-language autoencoder verbalization, decision-token logit attribution, and top-feature characterization) agree that scaffold and format features, but not medical features, drive the decision logits. Behaviorally, the multiple-choice penalty inverts under both structured and natural-language input, option-order shuffle rules out positional bias, and the gap is dominated by off-by-one decision (the model picks an adjacent acuity letter to the gold answer) rather than knowledge failure. Thus, the failure originates in the output format and not in the clinical representation.

</details>


### [188] [ExCAM: Explainable Cultural Awareness Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29897)
*Christoph Leiter,Haiyue Song,Hour Kaing,Jin Tei,Hideki Tanaka,Masao Utiyama,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ExCAM是一个专门用于评估大语言模型文化意识的评估指标，能够识别、评分并解释指令-输出对中的文化错误，在平衡测试集上达到80%的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文化意识基准依赖耗时昂贵的人工标注，且缺乏对自由文本中文化意识进行评估的专门指标，现有评估机制也较为陈旧。

Method: 提出了ExCAM（可解释文化意识度量指标），并构建了ExCAM40k数据集（整合9个现有基准并添加合成错误），通过与包括GPT-5在内的多个基线对比进行验证。

Result: ExCAM在平衡测试集上达到最高80%的错误检测准确率，超越了所有对比基线。

Conclusion: ExCAM为自由文本的细粒度、可解释的文化评估开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Evaluating the cultural awareness of large language models is crucial to ensure the fairness of generated text and the generalizability of applications across the world. Recent benchmarks explore cultural goods like food or values like behavior in stressful situations through the lens of question answering or text generation tasks. However, creating these benchmarks requires time-intensive and costly human annotations. Also, benchmarks that evaluate cultural awareness in free text are scarce and often rely on dated evaluation mechanisms. To address this gap, we introduce ExCAM, an Explainable Cultural Awareness Metric, which is, to our knowledge, the first dedicated evaluation metric that identifies, rates and explains cultural errors in instruction-output pairs. To train and evaluate ExCAM, we introduce ExCAM40k, a dataset comprised of nine existing benchmarks that we reformat and enhance with synthetic errors. Compared to several baselines, including GPT-5, ExCAM achieves the highest error detection rate with up to 80% accuracy on a balanced test set. Therefore, ExCAM opens the pathway towards fine-grained and explainable cultural evaluation of free text.

</details>


### [189] [Causal Interventions on Continuous Variables: A Case Study on Verb Bias in Steering Vectors for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29971)
*Zhenghao Herbert Zhou,R. Thomas McCoy,Robert Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对连续变量进行因果干预的方法，通过在语言模型表示中定位低维方向并编辑向量实现反事实目标值。将该方法应用于动词偏好（verb bias）这一连续特征，证明其在大语言模型中具有因果表征，且反事实编辑能系统性改变下游句法结构偏好。进一步分析发现，引导向量编码了可用于上下文学习的误差信号，但这些信号在下游生成中未被因果使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果干预方法主要针对离散特征（如语法数），而语言模型也需要处理分级（连续）特征。作者希望将因果干预扩展到连续变量，以更全面地理解模型内部表征。

Method: 给定激活向量配对与分级目标变量，定位该变量的低维方向，然后利用该方向将向量编辑至反事实目标值。

Result: 1) 动词偏好在LLM中具有因果表征，反事实编辑系统性地改变了下游结构偏好；2) 引导向量编码了误差信号，该信号与上下文学习中的误差驱动更新行为相关；3) 但这些误差信号在下游生成中未被因果利用。

Conclusion: 因果干预可以应用于连续变量，但将连续变量与上下文学习连接起来仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Causal interventions in language model representations have largely targeted discrete features, like grammatical number. However, language models must also make use of features that are graded. We introduce a method for causal intervention on continuous variables: given activation vectors paired with a graded target variable, we localize a low-dimensional direction for that variable and use this direction to edit a vectors toward counterfactual target values. We apply this method to a continuous feature that is well-studied in psycholinguistics, namely verb bias (which reflects which syntactic structures tend to follow a given verb). We show that verb bias is causally represented in steering vectors extracted from large language models: counterfactual edits to verb bias systematically shift downstream structural preferences. Verb bias has also previously been linked to in-context learning; in further analyses, we find that steering vectors encode error signals that could drive the error-driven update behavior seen in in-context learning but that these aspects of the steering vectors are not causally used in downstream production. Overall, these results show causal interventions can be applied to continuous variables, though connecting continuous variables to in-context learning remains a challenge.

</details>


### [190] [Adapting Multilingual Embedding Models to Turkish via Cross-Lingual Tokenizer Surgery and Offline Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29992)
*M. Ali Bayram,Banu Diri,Savaş Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbeddingMagibu-200m，一个面向土耳其语的高效句子嵌入模型，通过三阶段适应管道（词汇优化、教师模型克隆、离线嵌入蒸馏）在单GPU上仅用约4小时训练完成，以200M参数在STSbTR上超越300M参数的教师模型，在TR-MTEB上达到63.9%的平均分，实现了成本与性能的competitive平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的土耳其语句子嵌入模型受限于BERT-based编码器的512 token上下文窗口，且缺乏针对土耳其语优化的高效嵌入模型；同时，完全预训练成本高昂，需要探索更经济的模型适应方案。

Method: 三阶段适应管道：(1) 通过频率分析从40语言语料库中构建土耳其语优化的多语言分词器（131,072词汇量，支持8,192上下文）；(2) 克隆教师嵌入模型权重，通过均值组合token映射初始化新词汇嵌入表；(3) 使用余弦相似度目标在40语言Wikipedia语料上进行离线嵌入蒸馏，避免在线教师推理。

Result: STSbTR上Pearson/Spearman相关系数达77.55%/77.45%，超越教师模型（73.84%/72.92%）；TR-MTEB 26任务平均分63.9%（26个模型中第7），参数减少33%。训练时间约4小时，成本$5-$20。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过高效的适应管道而非完整预训练，可以构建出针对低资源语言的competitive句子嵌入模型，在显著降低计算成本的同时甚至超越更大规模教师模型的性能。所有资源已开源以促进可复现性。

Abstract: Sentence embeddings are a foundational component for semantic search, clustering, classification, and retrieval-augmented generation. This paper presents embeddingmagibu-200m, a Turkish-focused sentence embedding model that produces 768-dimensional L2-normalized vectors and supports an 8,192-token context window, far exceeding the 512-token limit of earlier BERT-based Turkish encoders. Instead of full pretraining, an efficient three-stage adaptation pipeline is introduced: (1) construct a Turkish-optimized multilingual tokenizer with a 131,072 vocabulary by pruning redundant tokens from the teacher's vocabulary and incorporating multilingual tokens via frequency analysis on a 40-language corpus, (2) clone a teacher embedding model while preserving transformer backbone weights and initializing a compatible embedding table for the new vocabulary via mean-composition token mapping, and (3) perform offline embedding distillation from precomputed teacher vectors using a cosine similarity objective over a balanced 40-language Wikipedia corpus. The resulting student model contains approximately 200M parameters and trains in roughly four hours on a single GPU by avoiding online teacher inference during training, at a total cost of $5-$20. Empirically, Pearson/Spearman correlations of 77.55%/77.45% are obtained on STSbTR, surpassing the 300M-parameter teacher model (73.84%/72.92%). On TR-MTEB (26 tasks), a mean score of 63.9% is achieved (7th out of 26 models), providing a competitive cost-quality trade-off with 33% fewer parameters than the teacher. To facilitate reproducibility and downstream use, all artifacts are released including model weights, tokenizer files, precomputed embedding datasets, and open-source cloning and distillation tooling.

</details>


### [191] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为潜在性能剖析（LPP）的框架，通过从隐藏激活和输出分布中提取任务无关的诊断指标，对LLM进行状态中心的内在评估，以弥补传统基于基准测试评估的不足。实验表明，不同模型在基准分数相似的情况下可能表现出截然不同的潜在特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评估过度依赖基准测试（如MMLU PRO、BBH、IFEval），存在数据污染、任务范围狭窄、与真实可靠性对齐不足等问题；基准分数仅反映模型输出结果，无法揭示模型如何处理信息、校准不确定性或组织内部知识，因此需要一种互补的、内在的评估方法。

Method: 提出了潜在性能剖析（LPP）框架，从隐藏激活和输出分布中定义一组标量指标，衡量模型的潜在表征和动态特性；这些指标具有任务无关性、尺度独立性和可解释性，能够揭示架构敏感的特征。

Result: 在0.5B-14B规模的8个LLM上实验表明，基准分数相似的模型在熵、适应性等潜在特征上存在显著差异；基于LPP洞察设计的合成探针能够与内在指标对齐，同时脱离基准偏差。

Conclusion: 建议将LPP与基准测试搭配报告，以提供更深入、可解释的模型行为理解，从而实现更可靠的模型选择、安全评估以及超越表面准确度的评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


### [192] [Recovering Diversity Without Losing Alignment: A DPO Recipe for Post-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30021)
*Vinay Samuel,Yapei Chang,Mohit Iyyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REDIPO是一种离线DPO数据构建流程，能从基础模型回复中恢复多样化有效答案模式，同时保持指令模型的对齐优势。在三个模型上，NoveltyBench distinct_k提升134%、33%和44%，且对齐性能基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式指令通常有多个有效答案，用户本可从中受益，但后训练阶段往往将LLM的输出空间收窄到少数规范回复，导致多样性损失。

Method: 对于每个提示，从基础模型和指令模型采样回复；用指令模型重写基础模型回复；经安全性和指令遵循质量过滤；构建偏好对，在具有相似指令遵循奖励的候选回复中偏好边际多样性的回复。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、OLMo-3-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，NoveltyBench distinct_k分别提升134%、33%和44%；MTBench、IFEval和Arena-Hard性能基本保持；HarmBench攻击成功率降低。

Conclusion: 通过精心构建的偏好数据，可以在保持后训练对齐收益的同时，重新引入基础模型生成中的多样化有效答案。

Abstract: Many open-ended instructions have multiple valid answers that users can benefit from seeing, but post-training often narrows an LLM's output space toward a small set of canonical responses. We introduce REDIPO, an offline DPO data-construction pipeline for recovering distinct valid answer modes while preserving the alignment benefits of the instruct model. For each prompt, REDIPO samples responses from both base and instruct models, rewrites base-model responses with the instruct model, filters candidates for safety and instruction-following quality, and builds preference pairs that favor marginally diverse responses among candidates with similar instruction-following reward. Across Qwen3-4B, OLMo-3-7B, and LLaMA-3.1-8B, REDIPO improves NoveltyBench distinct_k by 134%, 33%, and 44% relative to the instruct checkpoints, while DivPO changes diversity by 0%, -6%, and -4% on the same models. These gains largely maintain MTBench, IFEval, and Arena-Hard performance, and reduce direct-category HarmBench attack success rate. Ablations show that marginal-diversity pair selection and base-response rewriting drive the diversity gains, while filtering and quality-bounded pairing help maintain alignment. Overall, our results show that diverse valid answers from base-model generations can be reintroduced through carefully constructed preference data while retaining the alignment benefits of post-training. We release our code and data at https://github.com/vsamuel2003/RiDiPO.

</details>


### [193] [Give it Space! Explicit Disentangling of Positional and Semantic Representations in Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30022)
*Pierre-Antoine Lequeu,Camille Barboule,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过解耦Transformer中的语义、绝对位置和相对位置信息流，发现绝对位置子空间会自发坍缩为低维流形，注意头会分化成结构和语义导向两组，且标准位置编码无法稳健保持宏观结构信息，而解耦方法可改善语言表示性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置编码是Transformer表示序列顺序的基础，但位置信息如何被处理和存储仍不清晰。现有方法（如RoPE）在长上下文理解等任务上仍有困难，因此理解内部位置机制有助于设计更好的位置编码。

Method: 修改编码器Transformer，将处理流显式解耦为三个独立的流：语义流、绝对位置(AP)流和相对位置(RP)流，并将掩码语言建模(MLM)目标限制在语义流上，从而实现清晰的机制研究。

Result: (1) 孤立AP子空间自发坍缩为捕获文档结构的低频二维流形；(2) 注意力头分化为结构和语义导向两组，RP仅支持后者；(3) 标准位置编码无法稳健保持宏观结构：RoPE和RP仅弱编码，纠缠AP在MLM压力下在最后层丢失结构信息。解耦方法在65个语言现象中的49个上改进了表示性能。

Conclusion: 解耦的位置编码方法能够保留位置信息，并在语言表示任务上取得显著改进，为理解Transformer内部位置机制和设计更好的位置编码提供了新思路。

Abstract: Positional encoding (PE) underpins how permutation-invariant Transformers represent sequence order, yet how positional information is processed and stored remains poorly understood. Modern PE methods such as RoPE still struggle on tasks such as long-context understanding or retrieval \cite{chen-etal-2025-hope}. Hence, a better understanding of the internal positional mechanism could help design better PE. Building on evidence that positional and semantic signals occupy nearly orthogonal subspaces in trained Transformers, we modify an encoder Transformer to process three explicitly disentangled streams: semantic, absolute positional (AP) and relative positional (RP), and confine the masked-language-modeling (MLM) objective to the semantic stream. This decoupling enables a clean mechanistic study and yields three take-aways. (1) The isolated AP subspace spontaneously collapses into a low-frequency two-dimensional manifold that captures the structure of the document; (2) Attention heads specialize into structure and semantic-oriented groups, with RP exclusively supporting the latter; (3) Standard positional encodings do not robustly retain macroscopic structure: RoPE and RP only weakly encode it, and entangled AP loses it in the final layers under MLM pressure. The disentangled approach preserves positional encoding, which improves linguistic representation on 49 of the 65 linguistic phenomena of the Flash-Holmes probing benchmark.

</details>


### [194] [Who Am I? History-Aware Profiles for Student Simulation in Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30051)
*Zhangqi Duan,Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Simon Woodhead,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于历史条件的学生模拟框架，利用强化学习训练档案生成器和模拟器，通过学生历史交互数据预测对话内容，在数学学习平台数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学生模拟工作缺乏对过去问答或对话交互历史上下文的利用，无法准确反映学生知识状态和行为模式，限制了基于LLM的自动化辅导工具中导师模型的评估与训练效果。

Method: 提出双组件框架：1) 档案生成器总结学生学习历史；2) 模拟器基于档案预测学生对话回合。两个组件均通过强化学习训练，使生成的档案优化用于忠实的学生模拟。

Result: 在从数学学习平台收集的首个真实世界学生对话与问答响应数据集上，该方法显著优于各类基线，验证了历史信息、档案生成和强化学习训练的重要性。

Conclusion: 历史条件（基于学生过往交互）是提升学生模拟准确性的关键，强化学习训练的档案生成能够有效优化模拟性能，为构建更真实的LLM驱动自动化辅导工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A key part of developing large language model (LLM)-powered, automated tutoring tools is student simulation, i.e., using LLMs to role-play as students, which can facilitate tutor model evaluation and training. Existing work mostly focuses on within-dialogue simulation, which lacks context on student knowledge and behavior, partly due to not grounding in past student question-answering or dialogue interactions. In this work, we introduce the task of history-conditioned student simulation, where the goal is to accurately predict student dialogue turns by leveraging information in the student's learning history. We propose a two-component framework in which a profile generator summarizes a student's history and a simulator predicts student turns conditioned on the resulting profile. We train both components with reinforcement learning (RL), yielding profiles optimized for faithful student simulation. We evaluate our method and baselines on the first-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses that we collect from a math learning platform. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines, and demonstrate the importance of history, profiles, and RL training.

</details>


### [195] [HEART-Bench: Do LLM Agents Exhibit Human-like Psychology?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30058)
*Weihan Peng,Chenxu Zhang,Qianao Wang,Yuling Shi,Heng Lian,Qihong Mao,Jiahao Pang,Chunliang Feng,Bowen Li,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统性评估LLM智能体是否能模拟连贯、类人心理的基准测试，该基准基于大五人格理论构建了11种不同性格角色，并为每个角色整合了1,000个结构化自传体记忆，通过64个基于DIAMONDS心理学分类法的决策场景，评估智能体是否能根据其人格特征和记忆做出一致的行为决策。经人工验证后获得了包含673道多项选择题的基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体研究主要关注任务导向能力（规划、推理、行动），而很少将智能体视为具有完整人类性格的实体，忽视了情感维度的重要性。本文旨在系统评估LLM智能体是否能模拟连贯、类人的心理状态。

Method: 基于大五人格理论构建11种正交性格角色，为每个角色整合1,000个结构化自传体式情景记忆（分布在理论指导的成长阶段）；设计64个基于DIAMONDS分类学的决策场景（涵盖义务、智力、逆境、婚配、积极、消极、欺骗、社交八个维度）；通过系统人工验证与过滤，最终获得673道多项选择题。

Result: 经过系统的人工验证和筛选，最终获得了一个包含673道多项选择题的基准数据集，可用于评估LLM智能体能否整合其固有人格特质和自传体记忆，做出与其特定心理特征一致的行为决策。

Conclusion: 该基准为研究基于LLM的智能体中的类人情感、人格一致性和价值一致的行为决策提供了一个原则性且可扩展的测试平台。

Abstract: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark to systematically assess whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology. Specifically, our benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile deeply integrated with 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. To rigorously evaluate the psychological manifestations of LLMs, we designed a curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy, a psychological framework that characterizes situations along eight dimensions: Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality. By subjecting agents to varying scenarios, the benchmark evaluates whether they can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions that are consistent with their specific psychological profiles. After systematic human validation and filtering, we obtained a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs). We believe this benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [196] [UniSteer: Text-Guided Flow Matching in Activation Space for Versatile LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30076)
*Yingdong Shi,Ruiming Zhang,Changming Li,Zhiyu Yang,Kaixing Zhang,Jingyi Yu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSteer 提出了一种文本引导的激活流匹配模型，通过学习残差流激活在自然语言条件下的条件分布，实现对大型语言模型（LLM）的统一行为控制，包括行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念控制、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类，避免了传统方法需要为每个目标行为单独设计干预模块的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于激活的 LLM 控制方法通常依赖固定的引导方向或任务特定的干预模块，难以适应细粒度概念和组合约束，缺乏统一、灵活的调控框架。

Method: UniSteer 学习一个从自然语言条件到残差流激活的条件速度场（conditional velocity field）。推理时，通过流反转（flow inversion）将源激活部分传输到潜在状态，在目标文本条件下重新生成，再注入冻结的 LLM。同一模型还可通过选择重构能量最低的文本标签实现激活空间分类。

Result: 在三个目标 LLM 上的实验表明，UniSteer 在行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念控制、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类任务中均表现有效，提供了一个统一的接口。

Conclusion: UniSteer 通过统一的文本引导激活流匹配框架，解决了现有激活控制方法缺乏灵活性和通用性的问题，为 LLM 行为调控提供了更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Activation-based control steers large language models (LLMs) by intervening on their internal representations during inference, and has emerged as an effective paradigm for controlling behaviors such as persona and style. However, existing methods often rely on fixed steering directions or task-specific intervention modules, making them difficult to adapt to fine-grained concepts and compositional constraints. We propose UniSteer, a text-guided activation flow matching model that learns a conditional distribution over residual-stream activations from natural-language conditions. Instead of fitting a separate intervention for each target behavior, UniSteer learns a universal conditional velocity field in activation space. At inference time, UniSteer performs flow inversion by partially transporting a source activation toward a latent state and regenerating it under a target textual condition before injecting it back into the frozen LLM. The same conditional model supports activation-space classification by selecting the textual label with the lowest reconstruction energy. Experiments on three target LLMs show that UniSteer provides a unified interface across behavioral control, truthfulness steering, fine-grained concept steering, multi-constraint instruction following, and activation-space classification.

</details>


### [197] [Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking for Tokenization-Free Hierarchical Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30080)
*Thang Dang,Akira Nakagawa,Kenichi Kobayashi,Koichi Shirahata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应目标动态分块（ATDC）方法，通过课程学习渐进调整压缩率，有效提升层次化字节级模型的训练稳定性与下游任务性能，在FineWeb-Edu 100B数据集上取得与基线相当的Bits-Per-Byte（BPB）表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型存在词汇表设计复杂、OOV错误和语言特定约束等预处理问题，而字节级层次化模型虽可避免上述问题，但其关键挑战在于如何优化压缩率以提升模型处理字节数据块的性能。

Method: 提出ATDC（自适应目标动态分块）机制，利用课程学习在训练过程中从低压缩率逐步过渡到高压缩率，并建立目标压缩率与每最内层块字节数（BPIC）之间的关系以追踪块大小演化。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu 100B数据集上，ATDC层次化模型在BPB指标上与字节级和词元级基线竞争；相比固定压缩率模型，训练更稳定，下游任务性能更优，同时保持字节级处理的鲁棒性和灵活性。

Conclusion: ATDC有效解决了字节级层次化模型中的压缩率优化难题，通过动态调整压缩率实现了更稳定的训练和更好的下游性能，为无词元化层次模型提供了一种实用的字节压缩控制方案。

Abstract: Tokenization-free hierarchical models are emerging as a promising alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), addressing inherent preprocessing issues such as vocabulary design complexity, out-of-vocabulary (OOV) errors, and language-specific constraints. However, a significant challenge in these byte-level methods is the optimization of the compression ratio, a critical factor that dictates model performance for processing bytes data via chunks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking (ATDC), a novel byte-compression control mechanism designed to enhance the effectiveness of dynamic chunking within hierarchical architectures. Our approach utilizes curriculum learning to progressively adjust the compression ratio during training, transitioning from low to high compression to stabilize the learning process. We provide an analysis establishing the relationship between the target compression ratio and Bytes-Per-Innermost-Chunk (BPIC), allowing for tracking of chunk-size evolution throughout the training phase. Evaluations conducted on the FineWeb-Edu 100B dataset demonstrate that hierarchical models equipped with ATDC achieve competitive Bits-Per-Byte (BPB) performance compared to conventional baselines operating at both byte and token levels. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits more stable training dynamics and superior final performance across diverse downstream tasks compared to models using fixed compression ratios, while maintaining the inherent robustness and flexibility of byte-level processing.

</details>


### [198] [DirectorBench: Diagnosing Long-Form Video Generation with Personalized Multi-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30090)
*Jiamin Chen,Qianben Chen,Jiawen Zhang,Yidi Wu,Yuchen Li,Xiaokun Zhang,Wangchunshu Zhou,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DirectorBench是一个用于长视频生成的个性化多智能体诊断基准，通过80个结构化元数据、7个用户画像和40个检查点标准，从脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态和稳定性五个维度评估视频质量，能够定位检查点级别的瓶颈并支持用户画像感知的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频生成基准大多关注局部视觉质量、短期时间一致性或通用提示对齐，缺乏对工作流故障和用户依赖偏好的诊断能力。

Method: 构建了包含80个结构化元数据、7个用户画像和40个检查点标准的基准，从脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态和稳定性五个维度进行评估，采用检查点级别的瓶颈定位和用户画像感知评估方式。

Result: 跨工作流评估揭示了一个单元间瓶颈：过渡质量平均仅为0.256（最佳工作流为0.356），而提示级用户需求满足度平均为0.71。人工评估验证了DirectorBench与人类判断的一致性。

Conclusion: DirectorBench能够捕捉人类可感知的质量差异，揭示聚合评分所隐藏的工作流和用户画像相关的失败模式，强调了对长视频生成进行诊断性和用户画像感知的基准测试的重要性。

Abstract: Long-form video generation is rapidly moving from short, single-scene synthesis toward minute-long, multi-shot creation with narrative structure, cinematic control, audio, and cross-modal synchronization. However, evaluating such videos remains challenging, since existing benchmarks largely focus on local visual quality, short-horizon temporal consistency, or generic prompt alignment, and provide limited diagnosis of workflow failures and user-dependent preferences. We introduce DirectorBench, a personalized multi-agent diagnostic benchmark for long-form video generation. DirectorBench evaluates generated videos with respect to 80 structured metadata entries, 7 user profiles, and 40 checkpoint criteria across 5 dimensions: script, visual, audio, cross-modal, and stability. Instead of reducing quality to a single aggregate score, DirectorBench localizes checkpoint-level bottlenecks and supports profile-aware evaluation. We evaluate 4 long-form video generation workflows, 6 base LLMs, and 7 user profiles. Across workflows, DirectorBench reveals a between-unit bottleneck: transition quality averages only 0.256 and reaches 0.356 for the best workflow, while prompt-level user demand fulfillment averages 0.71. We further conduct human evaluation with 14 annotators to validate the alignment between DirectorBench and human judgment. The results show that DirectorBench captures human-perceptible quality differences and reveals workflow- and profile-dependent failure modes that are hidden by aggregate scoring. These findings highlight the importance of diagnostic and profile-aware benchmarking for long-form video generation.

</details>


### [199] [SEAL: Can Saturated Benchmarks Be Revived by LLM-as-a-Meta-Judge?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30104)
*Jiamin Chen,Yidi Wu,Qiexiang Wang,Qianben Chen,Yuchen Li,Yansen Zhang,Xiaokun Zhang,Wangchunshu Zhou,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEAL是一种通过自适应LLM元裁判和淘汰赛制来从已饱和基准中提取有效排名信号的新型评估协议，相比完整配对评估仅需约40%的调用量即可达到高排名一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型基准日益饱和，前沿系统得分接近而无法区分，但构建更难基准成本高昂，因此作者希望探索能否通过改进评估方法而非构建新任务来恢复已有基准的区分能力。

Method: SEAL采用淘汰赛制，将候选输出作为种子进入单败淘汰赛，每场比赛使用任务级原则加上自改进的检查清单标准，由LLM作为元裁判进行评估。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理、知识密集型问答和工具使用等多个饱和基准上，SEAL与完整配对评判的Spearman相关系数达0.83-1.00，top-1一致性达4/4，每任务仅需11.89次调用，而完整配对评估需28.00次。

Conclusion: SEAL在排名准确性与延迟之间实现了更优的权衡，是一种能从饱和基准中提取有效排名信号的自改进评估协议。

Abstract: Widely used language-model benchmarks are increasingly saturated, with frontier systems often receiving near-tied scores that standard metrics cannot resolve. Rather than constructing harder alternatives, we ask whether existing tasks can be made informative again through improved evaluation over the same candidate outputs. Therefore, we present Seeded Elimination with Adaptive LLM-as-a-Meta-Judge, a self-improving evaluation protocol for extracting latent ranking signal from saturated benchmarks. SEAL seeds candidate outputs into a single elimination and evaluates each match with task-level principles plus self-improving checklist criteria. We evaluate SEAL on multiple saturated benchmarks covering code generation, mathematical reasoning, knowledge-intensive question answering, and tool-use agent task completion. Across these settings, SEAL improves the ranking-accuracy--latency trade-off over competing protocols, attaining 0.83--1.00 Spearman agreement with full pairwise judging and 4/4 top-1 agreement, while requiring only 11.89 calls per task compared with 28.00 for full pairwise evaluation.

</details>


### [200] [Dial HEALTHDIAL for Advice: A Multilingual and Multi-Parallel Spoken Dialogue Dataset for Knowledge-Grounded Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30107)
*Songbo Hu,Yinhong Liu,Ej Zhou,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Xiaobin Wang,Alexander Fraser,Ivan Vulić,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HEALTHDIAL，一个大规模、多语言、多平行的话语数据集，包含6,000个信息寻求型对话（4种语言各1,500个）和163小时用户语音，基于WHO可信内容构建，用于RAG口语对话系统的开发与评估。基准测试显示各语言间存在持续的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建多语言口语对话数据集在方法上具有挑战性，现有资源在多语言、多平行的大规模构建方面不足，难以支撑RAG口语对话系统的跨语言评估与开发。

Method: 基于WHO可信内容构建4种官方语言（阿拉伯语、中文、英语、西班牙语）各1,500个信息寻求对话，录制163小时来自不同方言背景母语者的语音，并标注人口统计学和社会语言学变量。

Result: 基准测试揭示各语言间存在持续的性能差异，即使在高资源语言之间也表现不一致。

Conclusion: 发布数据集、原型系统及数据收集与评估工具包，以支持未来多语言口语对话系统的研究。

Abstract: Creating spoken dialogue datasets is methodologically challenging, and these challenges are amplified when the goal is to build multilingual, multi-parallel datasets at scale. This work introduces HEALTHDIAL, a large-scale, multilingual, and multi-parallel dataset for developing and evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based spoken dialogue systems. The dataset comprises 6,000 information-seeking dialogues (1,500 per language) grounded in trusted content from the World Health Organization (WHO) and 163 hours of user speech recorded from native speakers of diverse dialects across four official WHO languages: Arabic, Chinese, English, and Spanish. Each speaker is annotated with demographic (e.g., gender, age) and sociolinguistic (e.g., primary language, region of origin) variables. We report benchmark results across key dialogue tasks, which reveal consistent performance disparities across languages, even among high-resource ones. To support future research, we release the dataset, a prototype system, and a toolkit for data collection and system evaluation.

</details>


### [201] [CCS: Clinical Consensus Selection for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30131)
*Xi Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zaiqiao Meng,Jake Lever,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出临床共识选择（CCS）框架，通过采样多个候选报告并选择临床共识最高的报告，在推理阶段提升放射学报告生成质量，实验证明其优于默认解码和通用Best-of-N方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成多采用单路径解码，但固定MLLM的候选池中常存在比默认解码更优的报告，推理阶段的决策机制是未被充分关注的瓶颈。

Method: CCS是一种解码器无关的推理时选择框架：先采样多个候选报告，利用文本效用与基于图像-报告多模态嵌入器的放射学适应效用计算临床共识，选出共识最高的报告。

Result: 在三个数据集和多个MLLM上，CCS一致优于单路径解码和通用Best-of-N基线，尤其在临床指标上提升显著。图像基础效用与文本共识形成独立的选择维度，推理阶段仍有较大改进空间。

Conclusion: 推理阶段的报告选择是RRG中被忽视的瓶颈，CCS通过多候选临床共识选择有效提升了报告质量，且与模型架构无关。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>


### [202] [CorPipe at CRAC 2026: Empty Nodes and Cross-Lingual Transfer in Multilingual Coreference Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30133)
*Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorPipe 26 是 CRAC 2026 多语言共指消解共享任务中获胜的系统，通过改进的单一模型架构同时预测提及、共指链接和空节点，在 LLM 赛道和无约束赛道分别领先 2.8% 和 9.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与 CRAC 2026 多语言共指消解共享任务（第五届），该任务重点比较生成式大语言模型与专用系统的性能，并引入 5 个新数据集和 2 种新语言。

Method: 在 CorPipe 25 基础上改进，提出新变体架构：在单一模型中同时预测空节点、提及和共指链接；进行消融实验比较不同模型大小、空节点预测方法以及跨语言零样本评估。

Result: 在 LLM 赛道中超越所有其他提交系统 2.8 个百分点，在无约束赛道中超越所有提交系统 9.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: CorPipe 26 在多语言共指消解任务上取得了最先进的性能，代码和模型已公开。

Abstract: We introduce CorPipe 26, our winning submission to the CRAC 2026 Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution. The fifth edition of this shared task focuses mainly on the comparison of generative LLMs and specialized systems; additionally, 5 more datasets and 2 new languages are introduced. CorPipe 26 is an improved version of CorPipe 25, with a new variant predicting empty nodes together with mentions and coreference links in a single model. Our system outperforms all other submissions in the LLM track by 2.8 percent points and all submissions in the unconstrained track by 9.5 percent points. Furthermore, we perform a series of ablation experiments with different model sizes, empty node prediction methods, and cross-lingual zero-shot evaluation. The source code and the trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/ufal/crac2026-corpipe.

</details>


### [203] [A Dual-Path Architecture for Scaling Compute and Capacity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30202)
*Markus Frey,Behzad Shomali,Joachim Koehler,Mehdi Ali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了双路径块（Dual-Path Block），在同一层内并行提供深度子层（共享参数重复K次）和宽度子层（扩大FFN），通过独立逐令牌门控灵活扩展计算量和容量，在相同FLOPs下超越基线模型，且门控权重具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer以参数高效的方式扩展计算量，但在固定FLOPs下容量严格低于基线Transformer，缺乏同时灵活扩展计算量（深度）和容量（宽度）的机制。

Method: 提出双路径块，包含两个并行子层：深度子层（共享参数重复K次）和宽度子层（单次大FFN），通过独立逐令牌门控融合两条路径，实现计算量与容量的灵活分配。

Result: 在两个FLOP预算下，双路径模型在语言建模和下游评估中均超越iso-FLOP匹配的基线模型，且参数量更少。学习到的门控具有可解释性：功能词和词汇内容偏向宽度路径，标点、符号和算术令牌偏向深度路径。

Conclusion: 双路径块有效解耦了计算量与容量的扩展，在保持参数效率的同时提升性能，门控机制提供了细粒度的逐令牌路由分析能力。

Abstract: Looped transformers apply a shared block multiple times and have emerged as a parameter-efficient route to scaling compute in language models. However, at fixed FLOPs a looped model has strictly less capacity than a baseline transformer. We propose a novel dual-path block that can flexibly scale compute, the number of sequential operations applied to a hidden state, and capacity, the parameters available at a single step. For this we expose both axes as parallel pathways within a single layer: a deep sublayer re-applied K times with shared parameters, and a wide sublayer with an enlarged feed-forward network applied once. Independent per-token gates combine both axes and allow detailed per-token routing analyses. We show that across two FLOP budgets, our dual-path model surpasses iso-FLOP matched models on language modeling and downstream evaluations, while using fewer parameters than the baseline at matched FLOPs. The learned gates are directly interpretable and show systematic per-token allocation with function words and lexical content trend wide, while punctuation, symbols, and arithmetic tokens trend deep.

</details>


### [204] [GRUFF: LLM Pronoun Fidelity, Reasoning, and Biases in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30214)
*Fabian Mewes,Anne Lauscher,Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRUFF数据集，用于评估德语中代词忠实性（pronoun fidelity），涵盖四种性别一致系统。研究发现LLMs对阳性和阴性实体表现出强语法一致性，但对新代词（xier, en）表现不佳；模型对干扰项普遍不稳健，但编码器模型在德语中比英语更稳健；职业刻板印象在不同语法格之间相关性很弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代词忠实性研究主要集中在英语（语法性别有限且几乎无性别一致现象），缺乏对德语等具有复杂语法性别一致系统语言的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了大规模德语数据集GRUFF，涵盖四种名词性别一致系统和四组代词，用于测量LLMs的代词忠实性（即正确复用先前指定的代词指代话语实体，不受中间干扰实体影响的能力）。

Result: 1) LLMs对阳性和阴性实体表现出强语法一致，但对新代词xier和en表现不佳；2) 模型普遍对干扰项不稳健，但编码器模型在德语中比在英语中更稳健；3) 职业刻板印象在不同语法格之间相关性很弱，且仅在架构相近的模型中表现一致。

Conclusion: 语法性别对代词忠实性有显著影响；现有模型在处理包容性新代词时仍有局限；职业刻板印象的表现因语法格而异，缺乏跨模型的一致性。

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [205] [Do Language Models Track Entities Across State Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30233)
*Zilu Tang,Qiao Zhao,Gabriel Franco,Derry Wijaya,Aaron Mueller,Sebastian Schuster,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer语言模型在复杂实体跟踪任务中的机制，发现LM并非逐步追踪状态变化，而是在查询出现时在最后token处并行聚合信息；特别地，LM使用脆弱的全局抑制标签实现REMOVE操作，这种非增量策略导致多种可预测的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注无状态变化的实体绑定，对自然语言场景下复杂实体跟踪（含多次状态变更操作）的机制理解有限，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过行为实验和机制分析相结合的方法，研究LM在包含PUT、REMOVE、MOVE等状态变更操作的自然语言实体跟踪任务中的内部机制。

Result: LM不采用增量跟踪策略，而是在查询明确后在最终token并行聚合信息；REMOVE操作通过全局抑制标签实现，该机制脆弱且导致多种可预测的失败模式；通过消除该抑制标签可部分缓解问题。

Conclusion: LM用非顺序策略解决本质上需要顺序推理的实体跟踪任务；行为分析与机制分析可相互促进，前者为机制假设提供线索，后者帮助构建更全面的行为评估。

Abstract: Entity tracking (ET), the ability to keep track of states, is a fundamental skill that underlies complex reasoning. An increasing amount of work investigates how transformer language models (LMs) solve entity binding $\textit{without}$ state changes. However, there is limited understanding of how non-toy LMs address ET problems of realistic difficulties expressed in natural language. To this end, we investigate the mechanisms underlying ET in more complex scenarios featuring multiple state-changing operations. We find that LMs do not incrementally track world states across tokens or query-relevant states across layers, but simply aggregate relevant information in parallel at the last token when the query becomes evident. We further investigate mechanisms of individual operations ($\texttt{PUT}$, $\texttt{REMOVE}$, $\texttt{MOVE}$) to characterize this non-incremental ET mechanism. Surprisingly, LMs implement the $\texttt{REMOVE}$ operation with a fragile global suppression tag; this global removal mechanism predicts various failure modes that we confirm behaviorally. We provide a mechanistic solution of nullifying this tag to partially address this issue. Overall, our findings reveal that LMs solve a fundamentally sequential task using a non-sequential strategy. More broadly, our work illustrates how behavioral and mechanistic analyses can fruitfully interact. Behavioral results inform mechanistic hypotheses, and insights from mechanistic analyses help build stronger behavioral evaluations by predicting failure modes missing from existing evaluations.

</details>


### [206] [CommunityFact: A Dynamic, Multilingual, Multi-domain Benchmark for Misinformation Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30241)
*Sahajpreet Singh,Insyirah Mujtahid,Min-Yen Kan,Kokil Jaidka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CommunityFact，一个可刷新的多语言（5种语言、2个领域、15,992条声明）社交媒体错误信息检测基准，评估了10个LLM在不同推理能力下的表现，发现封闭输入验证依然困难，联网搜索效果提升最大，但LLM的来源选择策略与人类社区笔记的共识系统性偏离，且这种偏差可通过检索扩展或剪枝的模型特定机制弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态基准无法充分衡量模型在公开、快速、多语言的在线错误信息验证场景中的可靠性，需要一个覆盖更广、粒度更细且可重新分发的基准来评估模型在动态环境下的表现。

Method: 构建包含5种语言、2个领域、15,992条独立声明的CommunityFact基准，评估10个LLM在封闭输入、思考模式、联网搜索等不同推理能力下的错误信息检测性能，并分析来源选择策略与人类Community Notes共识的偏差。

Result: 封闭输入验证仍具挑战性；联网搜索带来最大性能提升；联网LLM的来源选择策略与人类社区笔记的共识系统性不一致，但可通过检索扩展或剪枝来缩小差距；不同语言-领域切片及联网系统使用的证据生态系统存在显著差异。

Conclusion: CommunityFact作为可刷新的基准，不仅可用于评估，还可将社区笔记作为训练信号，用于声明条件性来源建议器，从而改进对新型声明的事实核查。

Abstract: Misinformation verification increasingly occurs in public, fast-moving, and multilingual online settings, where static benchmarks provide an incomplete measure of model reliability. We introduce CommunityFact, a refreshable benchmark for misinformation detection in the wild, with three major goals: coverage, granularity, and redistributability. This release contains 15,992 standalone claims across five languages and two domains. We evaluate ten LLMs under varying inference-time capabilities, including thinking and web-search. Our results show that closed-input verification remains challenging, web access yields the largest gains, and web-enabled LLMs' source-selection policies are systematically misaligned with the sources human Community Notes raters converge on -- a gap that closes through model-specific mechanisms of retrieval expansion or pruning. We further find substantial variation across language-domain slices and across the evidence ecosystems used by web-enabled systems. Beyond evaluation, CommunityFact positions Community Notes as a training signal for claim-conditioned source suggesters that could improve factual verification on novel claims.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [FreeForm: Reduced-Order Deformable Simulation from Particle-Based Skinning Eigenmodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29318)
*Donglai Xiang,Vismay Modi,Rishit Dagli,Ty Trusty,Gilles Daviet,Anka He Chen,Nicholas Sharp,David I. W. Levin*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于再生核粒子方法（RKPM）的无网格约化阶弹性物体仿真框架，通过求解广义特征系统构造降阶蒙皮权重，相比神经场方法训练速度提升40倍，且仿真误差低于有限元方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约化阶弹性动力学仿真中，基于网格的方法难以处理复杂形状的扫描和三角化，而基于神经场的方法需要对每个形状进行单独优化，效率低下。本文旨在提出一种无需网格、无需逐形状优化的高效降阶仿真方法。

Method: 采用再生核粒子方法（RKPM）表示几何，通过在弹性势能Hessian矩阵上求解广义特征系统来构建降阶蒙皮权重，实现无网格的约化阶仿真。

Result: 相比神经场逐形状优化方法，训练速度提升40倍；与有限元方法收敛结果相比，仿真误差更低；能够处理网格和高斯泼溅等多种表示形式的物体，并成功应用于机器人仿真下游任务。

Conclusion: RKPM表示在约化阶弹性体仿真中兼具高效性和准确性，无需网格且无需逐形状优化，具有良好的通用性和下游应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a novel formulation for mesh-free, reduced-order simulation of deformable hyperelastic objects. Existing work in reduced-order elastodynamic simulation represents the input geometry by either meshes, which can be difficult to obtain due to challenges in scanning and triangulating complex shapes, or by neural fields that require per-shape optimization. We propose to adopt a Reproducing Kernel Particle Method (RKPM) representation, which enables the construction of reduced-order skinning weights by solving a generalized eigensystem on the Hessian matrix of the elastic energy. We demonstrate that this formulation not only leads to a 40x training speedup compared with the per-shape optimization of neural fields, but also achieves lower simulation error when evaluated against the converged results of finite element method. We show our simulation results on a wide variety of objects in different representations including meshes and Gaussian splats, as well as the application of our method in the downstream task of robot simulation.

</details>


### [208] [Before the Shutter: Aesthetic and Actionable Portrait Photography Planning in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30318)
*Ruixiang Jiang,Chang Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出3D美学肖像规划任务，在快门开启前自动生成人物姿态、相机、灯光和曝光方案，通过构建摄影场景图（PSG）表示场景可供性、主体-场景关系和照明结构，实现美观且物理可行的肖像摄影规划。实验表明该方法在人类评分和多模态大语言模型评估中优于基线方法，为从后期处理转向拍摄前计算规划提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肖像摄影计算方法主要聚焦于拍摄后的2D图像编辑（如修图、重光照），而拍摄前的摄影规划（人物姿态、相机配置、灯光设备的协调）这一关键环节尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建摄影场景图（Photographic Scene Graph），表示场景可供性、主体-场景关系和肖像相关照明结构；2) 在此表示基础上进行美学引导的比较规划，结合先前尝试和当前取景器观察结果来优化方案。

Result: 在多种室内外场景中，该方法生成的肖像在人类评分和多模态大语言模型评估中均优于竞争基线，同时保持了较高的物理合理性。

Conclusion: 本文工作表明计算摄影可以从拍摄后校正向拍摄前规划转变，为自动化的拍摄前肖像规划提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Portrait photography is largely decided before the shutter opens: the subject's pose, the camera configuration, and the lighting devices must be coordinated within the surrounding 3D scene. In contrast, most existing computational methods focus on post-production in 2D image space, such as retouching, relighting, or editing images that already exist; pre-capture photographic planning remains largely unexplored. We introduce 3D aesthetic portrait planning, the task of generating human pose, camera, lighting, and exposure plans that produce visually compelling portraits while satisfying geometric and photometric feasibility in a 3D scene. Our approach builds a Photographic Scene Graph that represents scene affordances, subject-scene relations, and portrait-relevant lighting structure. Built on this representation, we perform aesthetic-guided comparative planning over previous attempts and current viewfinder observations. Experiments across diverse indoor and outdoor scenes show that our method produces portraits preferred by human raters and MLLM evaluators over competitive baselines, while maintaining high physical plausibility. Together, our results suggest a path from post-capture correction toward pre-capture computational portrait planning. Project repository: https://github.com/songrise/Before-the-Shutter

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STHTD-MP方法，用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替代协方差度量，在Mirror-Prox框架下实现稳定的离策略时序差分学习，理论分析和实验表明该方法在特定条件下优于GTD2-MP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度时序差分方法的实际性能受辅助变量度量所诱导的几何结构影响显著，现有方法（如Mirror-Prox TD使用特征协方差度量）未能充分利用行为策略转移信息来提供更优的更新几何结构。

Method: 提出STHTD-MP方法，在原-对偶鞍点公式中将协方差度量替换为行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分；对辅助变量和原始变量使用单一学习率；对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤。

Result: 在标准随机逼近假设下证明了收敛性；推导了投影-预言机遍历间隙界；与GTD2-MP进行了精确均值算子对比；在Two-State、Random Walk和Boyan Chain基准上支持了条件成立，Baird反例为奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP在行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何结构时，其均值压缩因子可小于GTD2-MP，为离策略线性预测提供了更优的几何视角。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [210] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CCT 是一个 306M 参数的认知分类 Transformer，在 WikiText-103 上以匹配步数协议达到 21.27 PPL（GPT-2 Small 基线为 24.19），其中 84% 的改进来自 GT-Full 单纯复形消息传递；此外通过三个负结果发现了"结构/一致性二分法"经验模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过融合范畴论与认知科学的组件来改进标准 Transformer 架构的语言建模性能，并验证单纯复形消息传递（simplicial message passing）在中等规模（306M）上的有效性。

Method: 在预训练 GPT-2 Small（306M）骨干上附加源自范畴论和认知科学的组件构成 CCT；在匹配优化步数、数据、优化器和学习率调度协议下，在 WikiText-103 上进行微调评估；并通过重训消融（绕过 GT-Full 单纯复形消息传递）定位改进来源。

Result: CCT 达到 21.27 PPL，相比 GPT-2 Small 基线（24.19）降低 2.92 PPL（相对改进 12%）；消融实验将 84%（2.45 PPL）的改进定位到 GT-Full 单纯复形消息传递；三个一致性类先验（sheaf smoothing、adjunction round-trip、curvature regularization）均无效。

Conclusion: 提供了首个消融验证的证据，表明单纯复形消息传递能在 306M 规模上改进语言模型困惑度；发现了"结构/一致性二分法"：增加新拓扑结构的范畴先验有效，而执行一致性恒等式的范畴先验无效。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [211] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM生成的科学论文评审与人类评审的差异，发现两者对齐程度有限，且作者可根据LLM评审迭代修改论文，在特定场景下可使35%的论文获得显著分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的论文评审正在被各大会议官方试点使用，但需要对LLM评审的质量及其被作者利用的可能性进行系统评估。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文，从审稿人和作者两个视角进行实验：比较LLM评审与人类评审的一致性；评估作者根据LLM评审进行迭代修改（draft-revise workflow）能否提升论文评分。

Result: LLM与人类评审对齐程度有限，最佳情况下也仅达到合理水平，且不同提示词和模型下差异显著；作者通过迭代修改可在特定场景下对最多35%的论文实现统计显著的分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM评审存在被"博弈"（gaming）的风险，作者可利用LLM评审反馈定向修改论文以获取更高评分，这对评审系统的公平性构成挑战。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [212] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过几何视角将编辑式擦除重构为乘法参数更新，利用层间正交变换在闭式解下实现精确概念擦除的同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何，支持单/多概念擦除，在4.3秒内可擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编辑式概念擦除方法依赖加法参数更新，而加法更新会纠缠神经元的幅度、方向和角度几何，导致概念擦除与整体生成能力之间产生非预期干扰；同时训练式方法计算成本高，限制了可扩展性。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE），将编辑式擦除重构为乘法参数更新：通过闭式解推导层间正交变换，精确擦除概念方向的同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何；针对多概念擦除中的冲突约束，引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作。

Result: 在单概念和多概念擦除任务上，OCE在概念擦除效果和非目标保留能力上均优于现有方法，可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: OCE通过几何角度的乘法参数更新有效解决了编辑式方法中概念擦除与生成能力之间的权衡问题，且具备良好的可扩展性和高效性。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [213] [Frontier LLM-based agents can overcome the ontology curation bottleneck for natural phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28965)
*James P. Balhoff,Hilmar Lapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了五个前沿大语言模型（LLM）作为智能体策展人在表型自由文本到本体术语的自动标注任务上的表现。在相同的金标准评估下，所有LLM智能体的表现均落在原始研究中三位人类策展人的个体差异范围内，最佳智能体接近但未超越最佳人类策展人；所有智能体全面超越了传统的语义CharParser NLP工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表型自由文本到本体术语的标注（表型标注）是跨研究比较形态学数据整合的关键，但该过程高度依赖经过训练的人类专家，劳动强度大、难以规模化，是研究流程中的关键瓶颈。现有NLP工具（如Semantic CharaParser）与人类策展人之间的一致性远低于人类策展人之间的一致性，亟需更优的自动化方案。

Method: 使用来自Anthropic和OpenAI的五个前沿托管LLM，每个模型作为一个"智能体策展人"，在提供原始出版PDF、同一套标注指南、四个项目本体（UBERON, PATO, BSPO, GO）和验证脚本的独立工作空间中进行操作，利用Dahdul等人（2018）建立的金标准进行评估。

Result: 所有LLM智能体策展人的表现均落在原始研究中三位训练有素的人类生物策展人的个体差异范围内；表现最佳的智能体接近但未达到最佳人类策展人水平；所有智能体在所有四项指标上均大幅优于Semantic CharaParser。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM作为智能体策展人在表型标注任务上已达到人类策展人的水平范围，显著超越了传统NLP工具，有望缓解该领域高度依赖人工专家的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Linking free-text phenotype descriptions to ontology terms, typically referred to as phenotype annotation, is essential for the cross-study integration of comparative morphological data. This labor intensive process has heavily relied on highly trained human experts, which makes it challenging to scale and thus a key bottleneck. Dahdul et al. (2018) established a Gold Standard (GS) of Entity-Quality (EQ) annotations across seven phylogenetic studies and used it to evaluate three human curators and the Semantic CharaParser NLP tool with ontology-based semantic similarity metrics; they reported that machine-human consistency was significantly lower than inter-curator (human-human) consistency. Here we revisit that benchmark with five frontier hosted LLMs from Anthropic and OpenAI, each operating as an "agentic curator" within a self-contained workspace that supplies the source publication PDF, the same annotation guide used by the original human curators, the four project ontologies (UBERON, PATO, BSPO, GO), and a validation script. Evaluated against the same Gold Standard, every agent fell within the range of inter-curator variability of the three trained human biocurators of the original study; the best performing agents approached but did not reach the best performing human curator. Agents substantially outperformed Semantic CharaParser on all four metrics.

</details>


### [214] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VFEAgent是一个端到端多智能体系统，通过多模态视觉-语言处理和验证优先的代码合成，实现从输入图像和问题描述直接自动生成有限元分析建模与仿真，在多种工程力学场景中优于LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专家知识，现有结合大语言模型的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性。

Method: (1) 多模态视觉-语言多智能体管道，采用ReAct驱动推理从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，集成自我调试和回退机制以确保可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景中，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，该框架有潜力将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [215] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议旨在通过建立人本化建模与仿真的基准测试，指导AI工具向负责任和合乎伦理的方向发展。该倡议利用开源基础设施，对AI工具在定性模型构建、定量模型构建和模型讨论等方面的表现进行自动化评估。测试结果表明，AI工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复方面较弱；不同引擎和LLM组合的性能差异显著，没有单一模型在所有任务中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界决策需要AI工具能够构建仿真模型来支撑建议并使其可解释，但自动化建模工具必须补充而非替代人类专业知识。当前缺乏系统性的基准来评估AI辅助建模工具是否真正符合人本化和负责任的要求。

Method: BEAMS倡议建立了开放的数字和组织基础设施，通过开源sd ai项目实现透明性和广泛贡献。设立指导组（优先确定基准）和技术组（将基准实现为自动化测试）。实现了涵盖因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建模步骤建议和模型修复建议等多个类别的评估测试，并应用于支持定性、定量建模和模型讨论的AI工具。

Result: 不同LLM与sd ai引擎耦合时性能存在显著差异。AI工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量错误修复。没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型中占优，任务特定性和速度-准确度权衡至关重要。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议的评估框架有效揭示了不同AI建模工具的能力差异和局限性，为开发负责任、人本化的AI建模工具提供了系统化基准。未来工作将纳入关注偏见的基准，考虑替代视角和人本化用例。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [216] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对约12,000名Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹分析，发现用户与LLM的交互行为具有高度粘性，个体行为随时间变化微弱，且不同活跃度用户存在显著差异；同时指出WildChat数据集严重偏向高熟练度"权力用户"，不能代表典型用户交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对用户与LLM交互的描述是静态的，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的理解。

Method: 分析约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行比较。

Result: 个体用户轨迹的趋势比群体层面趋势弱得多；不同活跃度用户存在显著差异；WildChat数据集严重偏向高熟练度"权力用户"。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著；WildChat不能代表典型用户-LLM交互，这对该数据集的后续使用提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [217] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种"解释性审计流水线"（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），通过多LLM之间的分歧来检测公共意见分类中的解释复杂性，并在联邦USDA文档的1,260条公众评论上验证了该方法优于传统准确性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构使用LLM对公众意见进行分类时，传统基于准确性（stance accuracy）的评估无法检测不同模型对同一输入产生根本不同的分类结果，存在解释性偏差风险。

Method: 构建Interpretive Audit Pipeline，利用多模型分歧作为解释复杂性的诊断信号，引导人工审查；在1,260条公众评论上比较四种LLM的分类一致性；进行两阶段标注研究（独立标注+查看他人标注后修订）。

Result: 模型间的主题分歧大于同一模型内的提示词变化；专家评分标准压制了深层解释分歧但未解决分歧；人工标注者在修订时引入了合奏输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 对于LLM辅助的解释性编码，基于分歧的评估是准确性指标的必要补充。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [218] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示LLM在客观选择题上的准确性会因提示语气（tone）的系统性变化而产生显著波动，且这种影响高度依赖模型和学科领域，提醒用户不应假设LLM具有语气鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的广泛应用中，性能常因提示风格和语气不同而变化，但缺乏系统性的量化研究来探究语气变化对LLM客观任务准确性的影响。

Method: 使用两个数据集（50题×5种语气变体，以及570题MMLU子集×7种语气变体），评估四种经济型LLM（ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）在客观多选题上的表现，并引入路由框架解释语气如何影响内部推理模式。

Result: 语气效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型：部分模型仅表现出微小但统计显著的偏移，而另一些模型在不同语气间出现大幅准确性波动；不同学科领域对语气的敏感度存在差异。

Conclusion: 用户不应假定LLM在语气变化下具有鲁棒可靠性；研究为理解提示工程中的语气机制提供了初步解释框架。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [219] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过对72名高等教育从业者的横截面调查，基于DOT框架（设计思维与开放系统理论），探索了AI整合教学中从业者的信念、行为与制度条件。因子分析揭示三维信念结构（AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作与规划，α=.90），发现从业者认可AI的教学支持作用但坚持人类监督，实践中迭代提示与内容生成较普遍，而需求评估与反馈循环使用不足，且政策、培训与基础设施等制度障碍广泛存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中AI整合日益重要，但从业者对AI的信念、行为及制度条件的系统性理解尚不充分。现有研究缺乏将设计思维与开放系统理论整合的分析框架，难以描述理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 采用横截面调查设计（n=72），以高等教育从业者为对象；基于DOT框架（设计思维+开放系统理论）设计问卷；对19个信念题项进行探索性因子分析，识别出三维结构；同时调查AI熟悉度、使用模式、设计导向实践和教学信念。

Result: 三维信念结构（AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作与规划）信度良好（α=.90）；从业者对AI持积极态度但强调人类监督与批判性评估；迭代提示与内容生成是主要实践，而需求评估与反馈循环使用较少；政策、培训与基础设施等制度障碍被广泛报告。

Conclusion: 研究为DOT框架作为描述性模型提供了初步实证支持，揭示了设计导向理论与当前实施之间的差距；贡献了初步的测量结构，并指出了确认性验证与基于结果的研究方向。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [220] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可微信念对手塑形(D-BOS)，通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态微分，在信念空间进行对手塑形，在隐藏角色游戏中优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑形方法通常在参数、策略或价值空间操作，而信念操控技术依赖硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和），缺乏灵活性。希望让最优策略从环境奖励结构中自然涌现。

Method: 将观察者的信念作为塑形对手状态，通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态进行可微分化，聚合多个观察者的信念轨迹梯度来扩展至多观察者场景。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS outperform PPO和BBM，在混合动机场景中提升最大。

Conclusion: 信念空间公式化为对手塑形提供了有效信号，允许最优策略从环境奖励结构中自然涌现，无需显式奖励欺骗或合作行为。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [221] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD提出了一种将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间的方法，通过阶段式训练课程实现推理时直接传播隐藏状态，在保持与显式推理方法相当安全性能的同时，实现了12.9倍加速和22.4倍Token使用量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于推理的安全护栏虽优于分类基线，但存在高查询延迟和Token开销问题，难以用于高吞吐量部署场景。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过阶段式训练课程将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间，在推理时直接进行隐藏状态传播而无需生成显式推理链。

Result: 在10种提示和响应审核设置、8个安全基准上，COLAGUARD相较于Llama Guard 3提升8.24个宏F1点，与GuardReasoner的宏F1持平，同时实现12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍Token用量减少。

Conclusion: 潜在推理可以作为显式推理生成的实际替代方案，在提升安全鲁棒性和推理效率之间取得了平衡，而非将二者视为竞争目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [222] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个策略无关的执行与测量中间层，用于解耦事件驱动调度策略与工业执行环境，将执行不确定性转化为可结构化的监督数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动调度策略在工业环境中面临决策状态时序不一致、动作可采纳性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊等问题，限制了系统可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 设计了一个策略无关的执行与测量层：从异步事件流构建决策有效快照，定义标准化执行合约（明确动作可采纳性），记录策略意图、事务结果、物理执行与人工干预之间的偏差。

Result: 离散事件仿真表明：在所有观测延迟区间下均有分析收益，将无差别的执行失败转化为带类型和完整归因覆盖的结构化结果；在低观测延迟下操作收益最强，可预防可避免的执行错误。

Conclusion: 该层实现了决策语义与执行行为的分离，使部署失配可观测、可归因，并将执行不确定性转化为用于评估和策略优化的监督数据。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous.
